### FP-068 — Operator Portfolio + broker-truth positions + broker equity curve + reconciliation surface (READ-ONLY UI over paper broker + persisted ledger; money-path UNTOUCHED)

|---|---|
| **ID** | FP-068 (next-free confirmed by grep: FP-067 was highest, 2026-07-01). |
| **Status** | **W5 BUILT (2026-07-01, ACT-444).** W1 BUILT (ACT-438); W2 BUILT (ACT-439); W3 BUILT (ACT-442); W5 (Closed-today view — Broker-fills primary + Internal-closed stubbed honest-empty) BUILT (ACT-444). W4 (optional cache) remains PENDING. Charter turn was ACT-437. |

**FP-068-ADD-04 (2026-07-01, ACT-444) — W5 shipped (Rule-8 additive; prior W1/W2/W3 scope preserved verbatim below).**

What landed in W5 (all read-only; money-path UNTOUCHED):
- **STEP A — thin closed-fills projection + read-only edge fn.** New `supabase/functions/longshort-portfolio-closed-fills-readonly/index.ts` mirrors the W1 pattern: `createHandler` + `authenticateRequest` + `checkPermissionOrThrow('longshort.view')` + `productionClock.getWallClockTs()` (NO raw `new Date()` — wall-clock scanner CLEAN, 0 violations). GET-only Alpaca `/v2/orders?status=closed&after=<todayEt00:00>` via the shared `AlpacaPaperClient` (INC-77 paper-only allow-list preserved). "Today ET" boundary computed via a DST-safe helper (`Intl.DateTimeFormat America/New_York` — tries EST/EDT offsets, picks the candidate whose ET hour is 00; defensive 24h fallback). Filters to `status==='filled' && filled_qty>0`, CID matching `/^lse-.*-(open|increase|decrease|close|noop)-.../`, **exit intents only** (`close` | `decrease`). Broker-side derives exited-position side: broker `sell` = exited LONG, broker `buy` = exited SHORT (buy-to-cover). NO InFlightOrder / state-machine reconstruction — leaner projection distinct from the tick-path `AlpacaRecentlyFilledOrdersFetcher`. Also returns `internal_closed_lots` (longshort_lots WHERE status='closed' with realized_pnl/exit_price/exit_ts — empty today, expected) and `open_lots_for_match` (used by the UI to derive entry_avg per exit fill without a second round-trip).
- **STEP B — Broker-fills tab (primary, has data).** New `PortfolioClosedTodayTab.tsx` with two sub-tabs. Broker-fills panel: per exit fill — symbol + side badge, `close`/`decrease` intent badge, filled_at, qty, entry → exit, realized P&L. Entry_avg is aggregated across matching open lots on (symbol, side): `Σ cost_basis / Σ |qty|`. Realized P&L per fill = `sign · (exit − entry) · qty` where `sign = +1 (long) / −1 (short)`. Unmatched fills carry an amber `unmatched` badge (title tooltip: "Broker exited a position with no matching open lot — DW-207 evidence") and are EXCLUDED from the reconciled realized total — never fabricated into a match (typed-absence). Footer row shows `Realized today` = Σ matched realized with `(n of m matched)` flag mirroring W2 totals discipline. Explicit `BROKER-TRUTH` header note keeps it visually distinct from the internal-reconciled tab.
- **STEP C — Internal-closed tab (stubbed, honest).** Reads `longshort_lots WHERE status='closed'` via the same edge-fn payload. When empty (today), renders a labeled dashed-border empty state: "No closed lots yet — populates as the strategy closes positions (see DW-207 — the rebalance close path is currently lot-fed, so broker-orphans liquidated outside the ledger will not appear here). This tab lights up automatically once the first close writes a closed lot." NOT an error state — an honest pending state. When lots exist, renders a table with symbol / side / exit_ts / qty / entry → exit / realized_pnl.
- **STEP D — mount in Portfolio.** `PortfolioHubPage.tsx` gains a `Closed today` tab between `Internal ledger` and `Equity curve`.

W5 STOP-conditions honored: (a) READ-ONLY, money-path-UNTOUCHED — new edge fn is GET-only, NO writes, NO rebalance/submit/execute/lot-writer, NO migration, NO cron; (b) INJECTED CLOCK — `productionClock.getWallClockTs()` (scanner CLEAN); (c) EXIT INTENTS ONLY — `close`/`decrease` filter enforced at the CID layer; (d) side_sign correct — long exits use `(exit − entry)`, shorts use `(entry − exit)`; (e) UNMATCHED FLAGGED, NEVER FABRICATED — no cost-basis synthesis, unmatched excluded from reconciled total; (f) EMPTY-STATE IS HONEST PENDING — not error; DW-207 called out; (g) broker-truth visually distinct from internal-reconciled; (h) both `deno.lock` remain v5.

Files touched in W5: `supabase/functions/longshort-portfolio-closed-fills-readonly/index.ts` (NEW); `src/pages/trading/longshort/portfolio/useClosedFillsToday.ts` (NEW — hook + `findOpenLotFor` + `realizedPnl`); `src/pages/trading/longshort/portfolio/PortfolioClosedTodayTab.tsx` (NEW — tabbed Broker-fills / Internal-closed view); `src/pages/trading/longshort/PortfolioHubPage.tsx` (added `Closed today` tab); `docs/08-planning/feature-proposals.md` (this addendum); `docs/06-tracking/action-tracker.md` (ACT-444).


**FP-068-ADD-03 (2026-07-01, ACT-442) — W3 shipped (Rule-8 additive; prior W1/W2 scope preserved verbatim below).**

What landed in W3 (all read-only; money-path UNTOUCHED):
- **STEP A — new broker portfolio-history fetcher.** `supabase/functions/_shared/longshort-broker/alpaca-portfolio-history-fetcher.ts` — new `AlpacaPortfolioHistoryFetcher` on the shared `AlpacaPaperClient` (GET `/v2/account/portfolio/history`). Inherits the INC-77 paper-only URL allow-list at client construction (`PaperOnlyViolationError` per DEC-068 (f)+(k).8) — the new fetcher CANNOT be repointed at the live host. GET-only, no writes. Typed-absence reshape: broker samples where `equity` is null are DROPPED, never fabricated as 0.
- **STEP B — read-only edge fn.** `supabase/functions/longshort-portfolio-history-readonly/` — mirrors the W1 positions fn: `createHandler` + `authenticateRequest` + `checkPermissionOrThrow('longshort.view')` + `productionClock.getWallClockTs()` (NO raw `new Date()` — scanner-clean per ACT-441). Accepts `?range=1D|1W|1M|3M|6M|1Y|ALL` + `?include_spy=true` (off by default). Maps range → Alpaca (period, timeframe). SPY (when toggled) via `PolygonPriceHistoryFetcher` — typed-absence: on missing key OR fetch failure the fn returns `spy: null` rather than throwing (broker curve renders regardless).
- **STEP C — 4-series equity chart.** `src/pages/trading/longshort/execution/EquityGrowthChart.tsx` refactored from single-series `AreaChart` → `ComposedChart` with four base series + optional SPY:
  - **BROKER-TRUTH total** — solid `hsl(var(--primary))`, 2.5px — Alpaca portfolio/history equity (the paper account's own equity). Visually dominant.
  - **INTERNAL account_equity (MIG-121 snapshot)** — dashed `hsl(var(--muted-foreground))`, 1.5px — the two-witness overlay; broker-vs-internal drift renders visibly (the DW-207 pre-lot-ledger orphan gap is intentionally surfaced until liquidation).
  - **INTERNAL Long MV** — step `hsl(var(--success))`, 1.25px, `stepAfter` — per-rebalance cadence.
  - **INTERNAL Short MV** — step `hsl(var(--destructive))`, 1.25px, `stepAfter` — per-rebalance cadence.
  - **SPY (optional)** — dotted `hsl(var(--info))`, 1.25px `strokeDasharray="2 4"`, off by default via `Switch` — Polygon daily closes normalized to the first broker-equity sample (relative-return comparison, not dollars).
  - Legend labels each series unambiguously ("Broker-truth total (Alpaca)" vs "Internal snapshot (MIG-121)" vs "Internal — Long MV (derived)" vs "Internal — Short MV (derived)" vs "SPY (normalized to broker start)") so broker-truth is never confused with internal-derived. Footer note explains the per-side step cadence is "as of last rebalance" (not a data gap).
- **STEP D — mount in both pages.** Execution Equity tab keeps `<EquityGrowthChart />` (now the 4-series version — internal `account_equity` preserved as an overlay, nothing lost). Portfolio page (`PortfolioHubPage.tsx`) gains a new `Equity curve` tab hosting the same chart.

W3 STOP-conditions honored: (a) READ-ONLY, money-path-UNTOUCHED — new fetcher is GET-only + inherits the INC-77 paper guard, new edge fn is read-only, NO writes, NO rebalance/submit/execute, NO migration, NO cron change; (b) INJECTED CLOCK — the new edge fn sources `ts` from `productionClock.getWallClockTs()`, no raw `new Date()` (wall-clock scanner CLEAN — 0 violations); (c) VISUAL DISCIPLINE — broker-truth (primary) vs internal-derived (muted / success / destructive) styled and labeled distinctly; (d) TYPED-ABSENCE — broker gaps drop rather than fabricate; SPY `null` when unavailable; per-side step + "as of last rebalance" note; (e) SPY OFF by default (toggle); (f) both `deno.lock` remain v5.

Files touched in W3: `supabase/functions/_shared/longshort-broker/alpaca-portfolio-history-fetcher.ts` (NEW); `supabase/functions/longshort-portfolio-history-readonly/index.ts` (NEW); `src/pages/trading/longshort/execution/EquityGrowthChart.tsx` (refactored to 4-series ComposedChart + SPY toggle); `src/pages/trading/longshort/PortfolioHubPage.tsx` (added Equity-curve tab); `docs/08-planning/feature-proposals.md` (this addendum); `docs/06-tracking/action-tracker.md` (ACT-442).

**Grounded anchors (verified at HEAD `2ed3840f`).**

1. `supabase/functions/_shared/longshort-broker/alpaca-position-fetcher.ts` today calls `GET /v2/positions/{symbol}` (per-symbol) AND `GET /v2/positions` (bulk via `listOpenPositions`). Mapped fields: `symbol, qty, avg_entry_price, side, market_value?, current_price?, fetched_at`. **GAP:** `unrealized_pl` + `unrealized_intraday_pl` + `lastday_price` + `change_today` + `cost_basis` are NOT currently mapped — they are what Alpaca returns per position and are the source-of-truth for the operator's requested "daily P&L" + "since-fill P&L" columns. See Alpaca docs `GET /v2/positions` response schema.
2. `alpaca-buying-power-fetcher.ts` returns `equity + buying_power` from `/v2/account` — a CURRENT point, not a curve.
3. **NO** portfolio/history fetcher exists — the broker equity CURVE (`GET /v2/account/portfolio/history?period=1M&timeframe=1D` etc.) is genuinely NEW work.
4. `longshort_lots`: `symbol, entry_ts, qty, cost_basis, side, status, ...` — the internal-ledger source; `days_held = now − entry_ts` (display-derived, no schema change).
5. `longshort_equity_snapshots` (MIG-121, snapshot-on-fire, INTERNAL — `EquityGrowthChart.tsx` on recharts per DEC-069). **KEPT** and re-labeled "Internal (rebalance snapshots)". The new broker curve is ADDED alongside, never replaces.
6. `PortfolioHubPage.tsx` is an empty `HubEmptyState` placeholder ("Deferred — Phase 4-5"). `ExecutionHubPage.tsx` runs `HubTabs { Monitor | Equity | Attribution }` on already-persisted tables. Both use `HubTabs` (`src/pages/trading/longshort/hub/HubTabs.tsx`) — URL-synced tabs via `?tab=<slug>`.
7. Read-only edge-fn precedent: `supabase/functions/probe-alpaca-positions-readonly/index.ts` — `authenticateRequest` + `checkPermissionOrThrow(auth.user.id, 'longshort.execute')` + hardcoded paper-api host (INC-77) + GET-only + `method !== 'GET'` → 405. **All new W1/W3 edge functions MUST mirror this exact envelope** (extended to the shared `AlpacaPaperClient` for its paper-only URL allow-list guarantee, not the raw `fetch` shortcut the probe took).

**Independent scope (formed before reconciling with supervisor).**

**The three surfaces this FP delivers:**

- **S1 — Broker-truth positions view** (Portfolio page, primary tab). A read-only mirror of the Alpaca paper positions page. One row per open position: `symbol` + `side` badge (long/short color-coded) · `qty` · `avg_entry_price` (entry) · `current_price` (mark) · `market_value` · `unrealized_intraday_pl` (daily $ + %) · `unrealized_pl` (since-fill $ + %) · `days_held` (derived — see next paragraph). Empty state when broker is flat. **Explicitly labeled "Broker-truth (Alpaca paper)"** so the glance view is never mistaken for the reconciled source of truth.
  - `days_held` note: **Alpaca `/v2/positions` does NOT return an entry timestamp** — it returns only the average entry price. `days_held` therefore comes from the internal `longshort_lots.entry_ts` joined by `symbol+side` (a partial-cross-view: the ONE field the broker view "borrows" from the ledger). When the ledger has no matching open lot (broker-orphan case — see reconciliation), `days_held` renders as `—` and the reconciliation banner (S3) surfaces the mismatch. This is honest, not silent.

- **S2 — Reconciled internal-ledger view** (Portfolio page, secondary tab). One row per open `longshort_lots` row (or aggregated per symbol+side; see UX below): ledger `qty`, `cost_basis`, `entry_ts` → `days_held`, since-fill P&L = `(current_price − cost_basis) × qty × sideSign` where `current_price` **reuses the broker `/v2/positions` fetch already made for S1** (no double-fetch — the two tabs share one fetcher call per refresh). Labeled "Internal ledger (reconciled to broker prices)".

- **S3 — Reconciliation banner** (persistent above both tabs on the Portfolio page). The join surface. Match key: `(symbol, side)`. Three mismatch classes surfaced explicitly: **(a) broker-orphan** — broker holds a position with no matching open lot in `longshort_lots` (e.g. manual paper trade, unhealed corporate-action, ledger-drift); **(b) ledger-orphan** — an open `longshort_lots` row with no matching broker position (missed fill, external close, reconciliation-drift); **(c) qty-mismatch** — both sides present, `|broker.qty − Σ ledger.qty|` > 0. Banner shows a count per class + a chevron-expand listing the offending symbols. Green "reconciled" state when all three counts are zero. **This is display-only** — it never mutates state; the underlying reconciliation cron (`reconciliation-tick`, FP-062) is the authoritative healer. This banner just makes the current divergence visible to the operator between healer runs.

- **S4 — Broker equity curve** (Portfolio page + Execution → Equity tab). NEW read-only fetcher over `GET /v2/account/portfolio/history`. Params v1: `period=1M`, `timeframe=1D` (daily EOD points over the trailing month; sufficient for the operator glance-view and cheap on the Alpaca side). Response shape: `{ timestamp: number[], equity: number[], profit_loss: number[], profit_loss_pct: number[], base_value: number, timeframe: string }`. Rendered via the existing recharts surface (DEC-069). On the Execution → Equity tab, shown **alongside** the existing MIG-121 internal curve (two lines on the same axes OR two stacked panels — see UX below), NEVER replacing it: the internal curve remains the on-fire-snapshot audit trail, the broker curve is broker truth.

**W1..W4 build waves (each a separate authorization gate — this charter authorizes NONE of them; build turns are subsequent operator prompts).**

- **W1 — Broker positions surface (Portfolio page > Broker tab + Ledger tab + reconciliation banner).** Backend: extend `alpaca-position-fetcher.ts` `mapPosition` to additionally surface `unrealized_pl, unrealized_intraday_pl, lastday_price, change_today, cost_basis, market_value` (they already come back in the JSON; add typed-optional fields to `BrokerPosition` in `longshort-broker-interfaces.ts` — additive, no breakage). New read-only edge fn `longshort-broker-positions-read` (name TBD at W1) mirroring the `probe-alpaca-positions-readonly` envelope but going through `AlpacaPaperClient` (INC-77 guaranteed at construction) + `AlpacaPositionFetcher.listOpenPositions`. Frontend: reshape `PortfolioHubPage.tsx` from the empty placeholder to a `HubTabs` shell with three tabs: **Broker | Ledger | (later) Equity**. Build the two tables + the reconciliation banner. Ledger source: new hook `usePortfolioOpenLots` on `longshort_lots WHERE status='open'`. Banner join: pure client-side reducer on `(symbol, side)`. **Bounded**: no migration, no cron, no writer. Read-only edge fn + additive interface field + one page reshape + one shared table component + one banner component.
- **W2 — Refresh model + "last updated" stamp.** Frontend-only. React-Query with `refetchInterval: 30_000` on the broker-positions edge fn, `refetchOnWindowFocus: true`, manual "Refresh" button, `dataUpdatedAt` rendered as "Last updated Xs ago" per table. **Streaming (websocket) explicitly deferred — flagged as a future heavier option, NOT in v1.** Bounded: purely wiring + a stamp component.
- **W3 — Broker equity curve (Portfolio > Equity tab + Execution > Equity tab dual-line).** Backend: new `AlpacaPortfolioHistoryFetcher` in `_shared/longshort-broker/` implementing a new `BrokerPortfolioHistoryFetcher` interface in `longshort-broker-interfaces.ts` (additive) — `fetchPortfolioHistory({period, timeframe}, ts): Promise<{points: {ts: Date, equity: number}[], baseValue: number}>`. New read-only edge fn `longshort-broker-equity-curve-read` (same envelope as W1). Frontend: extract `EquityGrowthChart.tsx` internals into a `<EquityCurveChart series={[...]} />` presentational component; render broker curve on Portfolio > Equity tab (single series) and both series (internal MIG-121 + broker) on Execution > Equity tab (dual line, legend labels "Internal (rebalance snapshots)" + "Broker (Alpaca)"). No new migration.
- **W4 — [PROPOSED, NOT SCOPED IN v1]** additive cache table `longshort_broker_positions_cache` (RLS-first, per-user or per-operator, TTL 60s) IF the operator later wants to cap paper-API request rate under multi-tab open scenarios. Explicit STOP: **NOT built in v1**; interval refresh + browser-side React-Query cache is sufficient for the single-operator glance-view workload. Flagged so it does not surface as a surprise later.

**UX (independent — reconcile with supervisor's below).**

- **Layout: TABS on Portfolio, side-by-side is wrong for two 6-column tables that both want full width.** Tabs `?tab=broker|ledger|equity` (mirror `HubTabs` `?tab=` pattern already used by Execution). Default tab = `broker` (the operator's primary "am I where I think I am" glance).
- **The reconciliation banner sits ABOVE the tabs**, so it is visible from every tab — not scoped to one tab. Sticky-top would be over-engineered v1; static-top is fine.
- **Row content (per position, glance-optimized — matches supervisor's proposal, formed independently):** `SYMBOL` (mono, bold) · `side` badge (long=emerald, short=rose) · `days_held` (small, muted) · `qty` (right-aligned) · `entry → mark` (two prices, arrow between) · `daily P&L` ($ and % — color: positive=emerald, negative=rose) · `since-fill P&L` ($ and % — same coloring). Nothing else on the glance row. Row-click expands to show `market_value`, `cost_basis`, `lastday_price` in an inline drawer for the drill-down. **The supervisor's proposed priority is adopted verbatim** — it is the correct minimum.
- **Refresh model:** react-query `refetchInterval: 30_000` + manual "Refresh" IconButton + `Last updated Xs ago` stamp. **Streaming NOT in v1** per STOP-condition.
- **Empty/error states:** broker-flat → dedicated `EmptyState` ("No open positions at the broker") not a spinner-forever; edge-fn error → toast + retry, tables show last-known data with a stale badge.
- **Divergence from supervisor to flag:** the supervisor's UX proposal is not visible to me; the above is formed independently. Points where an operator-side reconcile call is warranted: (i) tabs vs side-by-side — I chose tabs (matches the existing hub pattern + full-width tables read better); (ii) the `days_held` field's cross-view borrow from the ledger — I chose to honestly render `—` for broker-orphans rather than compute a fake days-held from the broker's `avg_entry_price` (which has no timestamp).

**New-vs-existing boundary (what this FP adds vs reuses).**

| Kind | Add (new) | Reuse (existing) |
|---|---|---|
| Edge fn | `longshort-broker-positions-read` (W1), `longshort-broker-equity-curve-read` (W3) | `probe-alpaca-positions-readonly` envelope pattern (ACT-334); `_shared/handler.ts` (DEC-023 envelope, T7) |
| Broker code | `AlpacaPortfolioHistoryFetcher` (W3); additive typed-optional fields on `BrokerPosition` (W1) | `AlpacaPaperClient` (INC-77 guaranteed); `AlpacaPositionFetcher.listOpenPositions` (already exists) |
| Frontend | `PortfolioHubPage.tsx` reshape (empty→tabs); `<PositionsTable>` + `<ReconciliationBanner>` + `<EquityCurveChart>` extracted from `EquityGrowthChart`; `usePortfolioOpenLots`, `useBrokerPositions`, `useBrokerEquityCurve` hooks | `HubTabs` (URL-synced tabs); `EmptyState`; recharts (DEC-069); react-query (existing wiring) |
| Schema | **NONE in W1..W3.** W4 (deferred/optional) proposes an additive cache table only if rate-limits force it. | `longshort_lots`, `longshort_equity_snapshots`, `longshort_target_positions`, `reconciliation_events` — all read-only. |
| Perms | **NONE new.** All new edge fns gate on existing `longshort.execute` (or `longshort.view` if operator prefers — reconcile at W1 gate). | Existing `longshort.*` two-segment RBAC (T3). |
| Cron | **NONE.** No new cron; no cron modified. | `reconciliation-tick` (FP-062) remains the authoritative healer; this FP is a viewer of its results. |
| Money-path | **NONE.** | The full W paths of FP-056/FP-061/FP-062 are untouched. |

**Bounded ROI framing.** ZERO direct money-path ROI (no signal, no sizing, no execution timing change). **Positive operator-value ROI** (Tier A): (i) the operator sees the paper broker truth without leaving the app; (ii) reconciliation divergence surfaces immediately instead of accreting silently between `reconciliation-tick` runs; (iii) the broker equity curve becomes visible next to the internal curve — the two-witness pattern for equity truth (parallel to how DW-156 established the two-witness pattern for divergence detection). Zero silent-ROI-reduction risk because zero prediction/signal/sizing/execution logic is touched.

**STOP-conditions honored in this charter turn.** (a) NO build code — this turn is charter only, all W-waves gated on subsequent operator authorization; (b) MONEY-PATH UNTOUCHED — explicit invariant stated; every W wave is read-only display over persisted or GET-only broker data; (c) INDEPENDENT UX — formed before consulting supervisor; supervisor reconcile points explicitly named (tabs choice, days_held cross-borrow); (d) STREAMING NOT SCOPED — interval refresh is v1, websocket flagged as heavier future option only; (e) FP-068 confirmed next-free by grep (FP-067 was highest); (f) broker view explicitly LABELED "broker-truth" distinct from the reconciled internal ledger; (g) NO cron change, NO migration in W1..W3, NO new perms, NO writer surface.

**Cross-references.** ACT-437 (this charter); ACT-334 (`probe-alpaca-positions-readonly` — read-only edge-fn envelope precedent); ACT-325 (Execution Hub — the read-only-over-persisted-tables pattern this FP mirrors on Portfolio); MIG-121 (`longshort_equity_snapshots` — the internal curve, KEPT, re-labeled, NEVER replaced); DEC-069 (recharts sanctioned charting surface — reused for the new broker curve); DEC-068 (f)+(k).8 + INC-77 (paper-only-URL allow-list — the new portfolio/history fetcher extends `AlpacaPaperClient` and inherits the construction-time guard); FP-062 (`reconciliation-tick` — the authoritative healer this FP's banner makes visible between runs); DW-156 (two-witness divergence-detection pattern — the broker curve alongside the internal curve is the equity-side analogue); `supabase/functions/_shared/longshort-broker/alpaca-position-fetcher.ts` (W1 extension site); `supabase/functions/_shared/longshort-broker-interfaces.ts` (additive typed-optional fields on `BrokerPosition` + new `BrokerPortfolioHistoryFetcher` interface); `src/pages/trading/longshort/PortfolioHubPage.tsx` (W1 reshape site); `src/pages/trading/longshort/execution/EquityGrowthChart.tsx` (W3 presentational extraction site); Alpaca docs `GET /v2/positions` + `GET /v2/account/portfolio/history`; Constitution Rule 8 (charter is a NEW top-of-file entry — no prior text mutated).

| **STEP B — DEC-066 §(h) resolution (documented investigation; NOT deferred to fetcher commit)** | **Live read-only probe of `public.signal_observations` constraints** (run 2026-06-23 against the live external Supabase backend via `supabase--read_query`; the repo migration `supabase/migrations/20260605113546_…sql` is authoritative for the original DDL, but the live constraint set is what the fetcher hits): <br/><br/> **Columns (`information_schema.columns`):** `operator_id uuid NOT NULL`, `signal_id text NOT NULL`, `as_of_date date NOT NULL`, `ticker text NOT NULL`, `value double precision NULL`, `is_present boolean NOT NULL`, `gics_sector text NULL`, `computed_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()`, `carried_forward boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false`. <br/><br/> **Constraints (`pg_constraint`):** (1) `signal_observations_pkey` = `PRIMARY KEY (operator_id, signal_id, as_of_date, ticker)`. (2) `signal_observations_value_is_present_check` = `CHECK ((value IS NULL AND is_present = false) OR (value IS NOT NULL AND is_present = true))`. (3) `signal_observations_carried_forward_present_check` = `CHECK ((carried_forward = false) OR (is_present = true AND value IS NOT NULL))`. <br/><br/> **DDL-vs-live reconciliation:** the repo migration shows columns + constraints (1) + (2); the live DB additionally carries `carried_forward boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false` + constraint (3). This is the DEC-060 short-interest-carry-forward additive landing, NOT a divergence — both the column and the CHECK are additive and consistent with the repo's later migration trail. No constraint exists on `signal_observations` that the repo did not introduce. <br/><br/> **Admissibility verdict for a market-level row:** (a) `signal_id` is free-text with NO value-constraining CHECK / enum — a new `market_24m_cumulative_return` / `market_realized_vol_6m` `signal_id` is admissible with NO widening. (b) `ticker` is NOT NULL — a market-level row therefore needs a SENTINEL TICKER (NULL is rejected by the column constraint). (c) `value IS NOT NULL` + `is_present = true` for regime features (always-present under DEC-066 §(e) fail-loud) satisfies CHECK (2) AND CHECK (3) with `carried_forward = false`. (d) The PK admits one row per `(operator_id, market_signal_id, as_of_date, sentinel_ticker)` — exactly the per-`as_of_date` cardinality DEC-066 §(b) specifies. <br/><br/> **CONCLUSION: NO CHECK widening, NO migration on `signal_observations` or `combiner_feature_vectors` needed. Gate-3.2 "without migration" holds.** The only DDL deltas in the 3.2-b..3.2-e ladder are additive new rows (cron + `signal_registry`) on existing tables, not schema changes on the feature carriers. <br/><br/> **Sentinel-ticker convention chosen: `__MARKET__`** (literal string, double-underscore prefix + suffix). <br/><br/> **Rationale (collision-checked against the existing assembler + signal readers):** (i) Per-name assembly intersects `signal_observations` with `universe_membership` and DROPS non-universe tickers (verified at `supabase/functions/_shared/longshort-combiner/shadow-ranker-orchestrator_test.ts` test `(sorch-5) non-universe tickers in signal_observations are dropped`); `__MARKET__` is not a real ticker and will never be in `universe_membership`, so it is naturally invisible to the per-name pass — the 3.2-c regime-broadcaster will read regime rows by `signal_id LIKE 'market\_%'` on a SEPARATE projection, not by ticker. No collision with existing ticker-filtering logic. (ii) Rejected alternative `SPY`: SPY is a real ticker (the Polygon source ticker for the fetcher itself); using it as the sentinel would conflate the input ticker with the broadcast-target sentinel and would falsely match `universe_membership` if SPY were ever admitted to the universe (the universe excludes ETFs today but that is not a contractual guarantee — DEC-054 R7 gross-scaling could shift this). (iii) Rejected alternative `NULL`: rejected by the `ticker text NOT NULL` column constraint per the live probe above. (iv) Double-underscore prefix + suffix is not a valid US-equity ticker character pattern per Polygon / SEC conventions — guaranteed not to collide with any real ticker now or in the future. (v) Visually unambiguous as a sentinel in diagnostic SELECT output; sorts to the top under `ORDER BY ticker ASC`. <br/><br/> **AUTHORING-TIME CHECK named in DEC-066 §(i) — RESOLVED here (3.2-a), NOT deferred to 3.2-b.** The 3.2-b fetcher prompt INHERITS this convention as a settled input + cites this FP entry as the governance trail. |
| **Sub-ladder (build sub-steps gated on this FP umbrella)** | **3.2-a — THIS** (FP entry + DEC-066 §(h) constraint resolution + sentinel-ticker convention `__MARKET__` chosen + logged). **3.2-b** — SPY regime fetcher edge function + new cron `longshort.spy_regime.compute` (seeded DISARMED) + `signal_registry` rows for the two `market_*` signals + `REGIME_PRICE_HISTORY_LOOKBACK_DAYS = 730` constant + typed-fail-loud per DEC-066 §(e); writes to `signal_observations` with `ticker='__MARKET__'`, `is_present=true`, `carried_forward=false`. NO `FEATURE_ORDER` change in 3.2-b — the assembler does not yet read the regime rows. **3.2-c** — assembler regime-broadcaster: read `signal_observations WHERE signal_id IN ('market_24m_cumulative_return','market_realized_vol_6m') AND as_of_date = $as_of` (NO universe intersection on this projection); broadcast the values into every per-name `combiner_feature_vectors.features` jsonb at that `as_of_date` under stable keys; typed-fail-loud propagates to the assemble outcome. NO `FEATURE_ORDER` change — the keys land in the jsonb carrier, indices flip in 3.2-d. **3.2-d** — paired TS+Python `FEATURE_ORDER` 16→18 (`lgbm-inference.ts` + `training/combiner/feature_contract.py`) + `EXPECTED_FEATURE_KEY_COUNT` 16→18 in `signal-catalog.ts`, SAME PR per DEC-066 §(d); `assert_feature_order_parity.py` re-run; `feature_order_hash` flips (ADDITIVE-NOT-BREAK at change time per DEC-066 §(f) — zero artifacts exist; POST-PROMOTION LOCK takes effect once the first artifact promotes). **3.2-e** — trainer re-validation: contract-shape check only (parity assert + trainer dry-run skip-gate; no fit because T+10 labels haven't accrued per ACT-288); confirms `feature_contract.py` `feature_order_hash()` output is byte-identical to the new TS hash. | **In-scope (DW-106-c, third commit)** | Daily cron edge fn `longshort-short-interest-carry-forward` (cron-only `verifyCronSecret`; weekday schedule non-overlapping with the existing 21:00 analyst-revision slot; mirrors `longshort-momentum-compute` skeleton incl. three-event audit envelope). Inside the handler: (i) read the most recent published row per ticker from `signal_observations`; (ii) call `carryShortInterest`; (iii) UPSERT `is_present=true, carried_forward=true` row at current `as_of`; (iv) on the FIRST emission, UPSERT `system_config` key `dw_106_short_interest_heal_date` with `{"heal_date": "<today>"}` if not already present (idempotent — one-time stamp; never overwritten thereafter). Schedule template SQL + DEC-040 post-apply verification. **Implemented across c-i (orchestrator + manual fn; ACT-250/251/252) + c-ii (cron handler + sql/20 template + MIG-102 disarmed job_registry seed + `stampHealDateIfFirst` helper; ACT-253). c-d (operator schedule-apply + enable-flip + first-fire attestation) remains.** |

Stays separately deferred per the DW-108 register entry. (iii) PEAD #8 — has explicit decay with bound (60 TD), exemplar pattern, not modified. (iv) Backfill of pre-heal as_of dates — DEC-060 §(iv) forward-only. (v) Bound re-tuning of any kind — DEC-060 §(vi). |

Both handlers carry three deterministic skip gates in order BEFORE the orchestrator call, each emitting `.skipped` with a typed reason and performing NO write: (1) global kill-switch (`job_registry.__kill_switch__` row `enabled=false` → `reason='global_kill_switch_active'`); (2) own-row disarmed (`enabled=false` → `reason='job_disarmed'`); and on the RANK fn ONLY (3) **assemble-completion gate** — queries `longshort_audit_logs` for a same-`as_of_date` `.completed` row (cron OR manual variant) and fails closed if absent or on query error → `reason='assemble_incomplete_for_as_of'`. RATIONALE for the gate: the ranker's only input guard is `vectors_read === 0` (no partial-set guard); without the per-as_of structural check, a rank fire racing an in-progress assemble would silently produce a live book on a truncated universe. The 15-min schedule gap is only common-case timing. Audit envelope: `.started` BEFORE, `.completed`/`.failed` AFTER, `.skipped` from any gate, catch path `.failed` with `stage='orchestrator_throw'`; all carry `trigger:'cron'`. Two-ranking shape per CROSSWIND §1.4 (L105/107/112/114/119) + §6.1/§6.2 (two independent LightGBM models): a name carries `long_rank` AND `short_rank` simultaneously on the same `as_of` (e.g. rank 18 long AND rank 412 short); book engine reads `long_rank ≤ 20` / `short_rank ≤ 20` / `> 30` / `21..30` directly per §1.4. [3.0a schema apply LANDED at ACT-233 / MIG-099 — see ART-025; the 3.0a sub-phase is the migration commit, items (2)–(10) below remain 3.0b–d build scope.] (2) **TYPED-ABSENCE feature assembler** under `_shared/longshort-combiner/feature-assembler.ts` (3.0b) consuming `signal_observations` for the 9 live signals; emits per-name `combiner_feature_vectors.features` jsonb = {critical (#6, #7): bare numeric z-score; non-critical (#1/#2/#3/#4/#5/#8/#9): per-signal `{__value: <z-score>\|null, __is_present: 0\|1}`}; enforces §4.3.5 critical-exclusion gate (names missing Signal #6 cross_sectional_momentum_12_1 OR Signal #7 short_term_reversal_1w are EXCLUDED before fallback formula); §4.3.5 coverage gate (>4 of 7 non-critical missing → excluded; minimum 5 of 9 per L402 = 2 critical + 3 non-critical); excluded names persisted with `features={}` + `excluded_reason ∈ {missing_critical_signal_6, missing_critical_signal_7, below_coverage_threshold}` (verbatim MIG-099 `combiner_feature_vectors.excluded_reason` CHECK values; corrected from prior `insufficient_coverage` draft at ACT-235 — see `supabase/functions/_shared/longshort-combiner/signal-catalog.ts` `EXCLUDED_REASON` constants) + `coverage_count` for audit queryability (ranker filters `WHERE excluded_reason IS NULL`); GICS sector derived from first non-null `signal_observations.sector` per (ticker, as_of_date). **NO `Decimal('-999')` written to disk at 3.0b, NO `// allow-sentinel-fallback` annotation, NO `docs/banned-patterns.md` override entry, NO `check-sentinel-patterns_test.ts` single-site pinning** — all four repositioned to 3.2 per ACT-234 R1 typed-absence reconciliation. Reconciliation evidence (dual independent investigation Claude + Lovable against HEAD `1b23b44d`): §6.5.3 L649–651 ("the sentinel is introduced at exactly one place: the feature-vector construction function that assembles the 16-feature input to the combiner. All other code paths use `Optional[Decimal]`") — DB persistence is an "other code path" → typed-absence; §6.5.4 L657–662 (training samples include `value_i = Decimal('-999')` but the missingness profile is "captured per Phase 2 sub-phase and replayed at Phase 3 combiner training") → sentinel is re-introduced at train-time from typed-absence + profile, NOT persisted in the feature store; §2/L387 axiom 3 (`None` canonical absence; synthetic numerics on disk = SENTINEL defect class absent necessity-narrowed ADR exception — and no necessity exists since `__is_present=0` carries absence and -999 is bit-exactly reconstructible from the locked constant); §6.4 consumer reality — fallback formula `Σ(z_i × is_present_i) / max(1, Σ is_present_i)` reads `__is_present` and NEVER reads -999; only the 3.2 LightGBM model input reads -999. The single `Decimal('-999')` construction site therefore relocates to **the 3.2 in-process model-input construction function** (immediately before LightGBM `.predict()`; exact path resolved at 3.2 build PR) per **ADR-008a (supersedes ADR-008)**. (3) Fallback ranker under `_shared/longshort-combiner/count-normalized-fallback-ranker.ts` (3.0c) implementing §6.4 verbatim; writes `combiner_rankings.ranker_source='count_normalized_fallback'` on every row. The §6.4 formula `Σ(z_i × is_present_i) / max(1, Σ is_present_i)` reads typed-absence via `__is_present` (absent signals contribute zero to BOTH numerator and denominator — never reads `Decimal('-999')`), confirming the item-(2) typed-absence persistence is consumer-compatible with no downstream change required (ACT-234 R1). (4) Book builder selecting top-20-long / bottom-20-short per DEC-054 baseline; writes `combiner_book` with UNIQUE rank invariant; `combiner_book.rank_within_side` is sourced from the corresponding `combiner_rankings` row's `long_rank` (when `side='long'`) or `short_rank` (when `side='short'`) — explicit derivation eliminates the side-ambiguity at apply-time that the single-`rank_within_side` shape would otherwise re-introduce (ACT-232 R2 third stale item). (5) Idempotent persistence per T8: rankings + book write `ON CONFLICT (operator_id, as_of_date, ticker [, side, rank_within_side]) DO UPDATE` for deterministic replay. (6) Daily-post-signal cron cadence (union of signal crons; per-tick §6.4 cadence DEFERRED to Phase 5 live-trading per disposition (a)). (7) Edge function `longshort-combiner-compute` via DEC-023 envelope (T7); strategy-audit per T4 to `longshort_audit_logs` events `longshort.combiner.compute.{started,completed,failed}` + `longshort.combiner.book_published` (4th event added at 3.0a per ACT-232 R3b — Phase-4 portfolio-sizing trigger surface, decoupled from `compute.completed` so consumers can subscribe to book finalization independently; cheap to add now, expensive to retrofit once Phase-4 consumers wire to the compute event; register in `event-index.md` at the 3.0a build PR). (8) MIG-099 LANDED at ACT-233 (3.0a schema apply) carrying schema + RLS + grants + indexes; ledgered same-commit. (e) **Dual-date axis blind spot (the highest-stakes finding — newly surfaced by Phase 0 probes):** the original code keyed decay AND look-ahead on `transaction_date` only (grep: `compute-insider.ts:34` `age_days = (as_of − transaction_date) in days`; `compute-insider.ts:157,160,206` `ageDays(r.transaction_date!, as_of)`; `polygon-form4-fetcher.ts:91` `Used for decay (age_days = as_of − transaction_date)`). **There is no `acceptance_datetime` field in the persisted contract, and no look-ahead gate against acceptance.** Per Phase-0 lag table below, transactions become knowable up to ~5.95 calendar days AFTER `transaction_date` (the SEC 2-business-day filing rule spans weekends). An `as_of` computed at noon ET with `transaction_date` filtered to `[as_of−90d, as_of]` would have included transactions whose Form-4 had not been ACCEPTED yet at `as_of` — a silent look-ahead bias of up to ~5 calendar days for filings near the window-right boundary. The phantom-firehose bug (a) made this latent rather than active (the compute never saw real transactions), but the rebuild MUST close this axis at v1 — see DEC-058 §(b). |

Alternative `index.json` exists at same path; not used at Phase 0. |

#### §B2 — Raw probe evidence (UA discipline + CIK coverage + filing shape + Form-4 XML samples + dual-date lag table)

**Fair-access pre-flight (DEC-058 §(g) evidence).** SEC EDGAR fair-access policy requires a declared User-Agent identifying the requestor + contact; courtesy-caps ~10 req/s. Verified empirically:

```
# Probe 1: company_tickers.json WITHOUT UA → expected 403
curl -s -o /dev/null -w "HTTP=%{http_code} bytes=%{size_download}\n" \
  "https://www.sec.gov/files/company_tickers.json"
→ HTTP=403 bytes=1925

# Probe 2: company_tickers.json WITH UA → 200
curl -s -H "User-Agent: Lovable-Crosswind-Probe FP-050/0.1 (contact: ops@crosswind.invalid)" \
  -w "HTTP=%{http_code} bytes=%{size_download}\n" \
  "https://www.sec.gov/files/company_tickers.json"
→ HTTP=200 bytes=796479
→ TOTAL_ROWS=10416
→ sample: NVDA(CIK 1045810), GOOGL(CIK 1652044), AAPL(CIK 320193)
```

**Form-4 XML field-to-compute-contract map (DEC-058 §(a)-(e) inputs).**

| Compute need (§4.4.4) | Form-4 XML XPath | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `transaction_code` (P, S, M, A, C, G, F, ...) | `nonDerivativeTable/nonDerivativeTransaction/transactionCoding/transactionCode` | IN-set per §(a) binding. |
| `transaction_date` (decay anchor) | `nonDerivativeTable/nonDerivativeTransaction/transactionDate/value` | Original (failed) build's decay key — preserved as decay axis per §(b). |
| `transaction_shares` | `transactionAmounts/transactionShares/value` | |
| `transaction_price_per_share` | `transactionAmounts/transactionPricePerShare/value` | `0` for code M/A (no cash price). |
| `acquired_disposed` (sign) | `transactionAmounts/transactionAcquiredDisposedCode/value` | A=acquired, D=disposed. |
| `direct_or_indirect_ownership` | `ownershipNature/directOrIndirectOwnership/value` | Per §4.4.4 not weighted; recorded for audit. |
| `is_officer` / `is_director` / `is_ten_percent_owner` | `reportingOwner/reportingOwnerRelationship/{isOfficer,isDirector,isTenPercentOwner}` | Role-weight tier inputs per §4.4.4 + DEC-044. |
| `officer_title` (NEO-heuristic input) | `reportingOwner/reportingOwnerRelationship/officerTitle` | DEC-044 `classifyRoleWeight` consumes this. |
| `10b5_1_indicator` | Free-text scan for `/10b5[- ]?1/i` in the XML body (footnote text) | Form 4 has NO structured 10b5-1 flag; check-box at form level + footnote prose. Phase 0 evidence: 5/5 AMZN filings carried the mention → DEC-058 §(c) detector design question. |
| `issuer_cik` | `issuer/issuerCik` | Cross-check with `company_tickers.json` lookup. |
| `accession_number` | (URL path) | Idempotency key — see DEC-058 §(h). |
| `acceptance_datetime` | NOT in XML — from `submissions/CIK{cik}.json` `filings.recent.acceptanceDateTime[i]` | **The dual-date axis — DEC-058 §(b) binding.** |

**THE DUAL-DATE AXIS — lag distribution table (10 filings; 9 with non-derivative tx; 1 derivative-only).**

| CIK | Accession | `transactionDate` (min) | `periodOfReport` | `acceptanceDateTime` | Lag (cal days) | Codes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1018724 | 0001018724-26-000020 | 2026-05-21 | 2026-05-21 | 2026-05-26T22:33:10Z | **5.94** | S, M |
| 1018724 | 0001936006-26-000018 | 2026-06-01 | 2026-06-01 | 2026-06-03T20:27:07Z | **2.85** | S |
| 1018724 | 0001374545-26-000008 | 2026-05-21 | 2026-05-21 | 2026-05-26T22:47:08Z | **5.95** | M, S |
| 1018724 | 0002024813-26-000008 | 2026-05-21 | 2026-05-21 | 2026-05-26T22:39:49Z | **5.94** | S, M |
| 1018724 | 0001557979-26-000006 | 2026-05-21 | 2026-05-21 | 2026-05-26T22:26:07Z | **5.93** | S, M |
| 5272    | 0002025132-26-000006 | 2026-06-03 | 2026-06-03 | 2026-06-03T21:05:52Z | **0.88** | F |
| 5272    | 0002076280-26-000005 | (none — derivative-only) | 2026-06-01 | 2026-06-01T20:58:59Z | n/a | (no ND) |
| 1467623 | 0002060166-26-000012 | 2026-06-01 | 2026-06-01 | 2026-06-03T20:47:24Z | **2.87** | S, A |
| 63276   | 0001967445-26-000004 | 2026-05-29 | 2026-05-29 | 2026-06-02T21:30:45Z | **4.90** | M, F |
| 1037646 | 0001037646-26-000038 | 2026-05-12 | 2026-05-12 | 2026-05-14T14:48:04Z | **2.62** | A |

**Lag stats (n=9 with ND tx):** min=0.88d, max=5.95d, median≈3d, mean≈4.2d. **All filings spec-compliant in business-day terms** (the 5.93-5.95d cluster spans Thu→Tue = 2 business days). **Implication:** an `as_of` at noon ET with `transaction_date ∈ [as_of−90d, as_of]` includes transactions whose Form-4 was not yet accepted at `as_of` — confirmed look-ahead bias up to ~6 calendar days. This is the failure mode the ORIGINAL build's `compute-insider.ts:34,157,160,206` + `polygon-form4-fetcher.ts:91` blind to. DEC-058 §(b) closes it.

**Architecture-fork arithmetic (per-CIK vs daily-feed; DEC-058 §(i) binding).**

| Branch | Per-fire cost (incremental, daily after-close cadence) | Per-fire cost (90-day backfill) | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Per-CIK** (839 universe × 1 submissions.json) | 839 reqs @ ≤5 rps self-imposed → ~168 s wall + per-match XML fetch (Phase-0 AMZN: 526 Form-4 in `recent` → most names will have ≪10 Form-4 in last 24h). Estimate: 839 + ~30 XML ≈ 174 s. | 839 + ~7,500 XML ≈ 1,667 s ≈ 28 min sequential. Backfill is one-shot. | Authoritative per-CIK; no client-side filtering of an out-of-universe firehose. CIK-mapping quirks per §(f). |
| **Daily-feed** (1 daily form.idx × 1-N days) | 1 idx (~0.9 MB) + filter to in-universe CIKs (~5% of 1,667 ≈ 83) + 83 XML fetches @ 5 rps ≈ 17 s + 1 s idx ≈ 18 s. | 90 idx + ~7,500 XML ≈ 1,500 s ≈ 25 min. | One file/day; deterministic shape. **Strongly favors incremental.** Per-CIK option still useful for ad-hoc backfill of a single name. |

**Phase-0 leaning:** daily-feed primary for incremental; per-CIK retained as a focused-lookup tool. Both bounds within the 600 s `job_registry.timeout_seconds`. The FP-045 queue-engine availability is noted but NOT obviously needed at v1 wall-clock estimates — DEC-058 §(i) binding question is whether the daily-feed primary is single-invocation or queue-paged.

#### §B3 — DEC-058 skeleton (every binding is a QUESTION for operator ratification; UNRATIFIED)

| Binding | Question for operator | Failure mode addressed | Phase-0 evidence anchor |
|---|---|---|---|
| **§(a) Transaction-code IN-set** | Propose: **IN = {P (open-market purchase), S (open-market sale)}** at v1, exactly per §4.4.4 verbatim. **OUT = {M (option exercise), C (conversion), A (RSU vest / award), G (gift), F (tax-withheld), I (discretionary)}**, also per §4.4.4 verbatim. **Named deviation:** v1 reads §4.4.4 strictly — exercise-and-sell sequences (the AMZN cluster of M-then-S) contribute ONLY the S leg, NOT the option exercise; this matches §4.4.4 but operator should confirm whether the M-leg's strike-vs-market spread carries information that warrants a future enhancement-FP. | INC-70 phantom signal (the original "code filter" was a client-side post-firehose filter against a wrong data set; the rebuild's code filter is applied against authoritative per-CIK data, so the IN-set actually means something). | All 5 AMZN Form-4 samples in §B2 — every filing carries M+S pairs, no pure P; the M-leg is OUT per §4.4.4, S-leg is IN iff not 10b5-1. |
| **§(b) Dual-date axis (HIGHEST-STAKES)** | Two options. **Option A (conservative; proposed):** persist BOTH `transaction_date` AND `acceptance_datetime` per row; the look-ahead gate keys on `acceptance_datetime ≤ as_of` (what was knowable); decay age uses `transaction_date` (per spec §4.4.4 `age_days = (as_of − transaction_date)`). **Option B (simpler v1; operator approximation):** acceptance-gate AND decay both keyed on `acceptance_datetime` — named approximation that shifts decay anchor by the lag distribution (mean ~4.2d, max ~6d) and over-decays transactions by up to ~30% of a half-life. Either option closes the look-ahead blind spot the original build silently violated. | **The dual-date blind spot newly surfaced at Phase 0 history-recovery** — `compute-insider.ts:34,157,160,206` + `polygon-form4-fetcher.ts:91` keyed everything on `transaction_date` only; no `acceptance_datetime` in the persisted contract. | §B2 dual-date lag table (n=9): min 0.88d, max 5.95d, median ≈3d. |
| **§(c) 10b5-1 plan transactions** | Propose **EXCLUDE** all `S`-coded transactions on filings carrying any 10b5-1 mention (the §4.4.4 "discretionary sales, excluding 10b5-1" clause). Detector: free-text scan for `/10b5[- ]?1/i` in the Form-4 XML body (Form 4 has no structured 10b5-1 flag at the per-transaction level — the indication is a form-level checkbox + free-text footnote prose). **Reliability concern:** the AMZN sample showed 5/5 filings with the mention, suggesting either (a) AMZN executives universally trade via 10b5-1 (plausible; Big-Tech disclosure norm), or (b) the mention surfaces even when only SOME tx rows are 10b5-1-covered (would over-exclude). Operator confirm the conservative-over-exclusion stance for v1, with a footnote-position upgrade logged as a DW if Phase-7 IC ablation shows S-exclusion drags signal. | Spec compliance — §4.4.4 explicit exclusion. Implicit: 10b5-1 sales are pre-scheduled and structurally carry no insider-information edge. | All 5 AMZN samples in §B2 — all carry `10b5-1 mention: True`. |
| **§(d) Role weighting** | Spec §4.4.4 verbatim: **CEO/CFO 1.0; NEOs 0.7; Section 16 officers 0.4; independent directors 0.3; 10%+ owners 0.5.** FP-042 already implemented this via DEC-044's title-heuristic `classifyRoleWeight` (NEO=0.7 approximated by title-string classifier; DEF-14A authoritative enrichment deferred to DW-093). **Reuse as-is** per ACT-156 contract preservation. Operator confirm no new role-weight bindings at v1. | Contract preservation (ACT-156: FP-042 compute / classifier / filter / z-score is correct and reused as-is). DW-093 unchanged. | §B2 AMZN samples carry distinct `officerTitle` strings: "CEO Worldwide Amazon Stores", "President and CEO", "CEO Amazon Web Services", "Senior Vice President", "Senior Vice President" — DEC-044's heuristic table consumes these unchanged. |
| **§(e) Formula / size semantics** | Spec §4.4.4 verbatim: `raw_signal_N = sum_over_90d(shares × price × sign × role_weight × exp(-age_days / 14)) / market_cap`. Where `shares × price` = dollar value, `sign = +1 for A (acquired) on code P, −1 for D (disposed) on code S` (after §(a) IN-set filter). Existing FP-042 `compute-insider.ts` implements this contract; **reuse as-is** per ACT-156. | Contract preservation; INC-70 left compute correct, only data layer wrong. | n/a (formula-level; spec-cited). |
| **§(f) CIK-mapping maintenance + unmapped-name handling** | Two sub-bindings. **(f1) Refresh cadence:** `company_tickers.json` snapshot refreshed at universe-refresh cadence (quarterly per current plan) OR on every fire (10,416-row 800 KB fetch ≤1 s — negligible). Propose: **fetch-per-fire** for staleness safety. **(f2) Mapping override table:** for ticker conflicts (Phase-0 evidence: `NXT` resolves to Nextpower CIK 1852131 but S&P-500 NXT is Nextracker CIK 1953967), maintain a small operator-curated override map in code (`INSIDER_CIK_OVERRIDES: Record<ticker, cik>`). Unmapped names emit typed `skip='ticker_to_cik_unresolved'` per the FP-042 typed-skip discipline — never silent. | INC-70 made silent ticker→data mismatch the failure shape; we close it with explicit typed skip when ticker can't resolve. | §B2 CIK coverage block — 15/15 resolved BUT one mapping conflict (NXT) found. |
| **§(g) UA + self-imposed RPS cap** | Spec the UA header constant in the fetcher: `User-Agent: Lovable-Crosswind/{module_version} (contact: <ops_email>)` — operator provides `<ops_email>` as a new secret `EDGAR_CONTACT_EMAIL` (NOT a credential; an SEC fair-access identifier). **Self-imposed RPS cap: 5 rps** (half SEC's published 10 rps headroom — DEC-034 chokepoint discipline). Pre-flight check at fetcher construction: a no-UA probe MUST return 403 (proves we're talking to the right endpoint with the right policy); a with-UA probe MUST return 200. | Phase-0 evidence: no-UA returns 403; with-UA returns 200. Without this binding the fetcher fails 100% silently the first time it's invoked. | §B2 fair-access pre-flight block. |
| **§(h) Form 4/A amendment policy** | Form 4/A = amendment to a prior Form 4 (corrects errors). Daily-index 2026-06-11 evidence: 6 Form-4/A vs 1,667 Form-4 → ~0.36% rate. Propose: **treat 4/A identically to 4** at v1 (same XML schema; the amendment supersedes the original transaction record by accession number). Idempotency: persist per row `idempotency_key = (issuer_cik, accession_number, transaction_seq)`; on Form 4/A re-fetch, the new accession produces new rows that ON-CONFLICT-DO-NOTHING; the original Form 4's rows remain in the table (audit preserved) and the compute layer should prefer the most-recent accession for the same (issuer_cik, owner_cik, transaction_date) tuple — operator confirm or counter-propose. | Idempotency requirement (Constitution / FP-045 precedent). Original FP-042 had no amendment policy because the phantom firehose made it moot. | §B2 daily-index 6 4/A out of 1,667 → low but non-zero rate. |
| **§(i) Architecture branch (per-CIK vs daily-feed)** | Per §B2 arithmetic: daily-feed primary (~18 s/fire incremental, ~25 min backfill) vs per-CIK (~174 s/fire incremental, ~28 min backfill). Both fit `job_registry.timeout_seconds=600` at incremental cadence; backfill is one-shot via manual handler. Propose: **daily-feed primary + per-CIK fallback** for focused lookups, single-invocation (NOT queue) — both bounds within FP-047 single-invocation envelope. If a future cadence change to intraday-15-min surfaces, revisit FP-045 queue. | INC-70's "per-ticker fan-out across a non-authoritative firehose" was the worst of both worlds; daily-feed gives us a single authoritative index. | §B2 architecture-fork arithmetic table. |
| **§(j) Cross-signal consistency with DEC-057 §(c)** | DEC-057 §(c) ratified ADDITIVE independence: §4.4.9 Tier-2 "significant insider transaction" overlaps Signal #4 (`insider_transactions_90d`); both signals score the same event independently by design (catalyst measures decayed event-presence; insider measures dollar-weighted magnitude). **Record consistency requirement:** any change to Signal #4's IN-set per §(a) MUST NOT silently change the catalyst classifier's "significant insider transaction" Tier-2 detection (currently keyword-derived per DEC-057 §(g); structurally orthogonal — confirmed). | Double-count prevention by explicit design choice rather than silent gating. | DEC-057 §(c) text + Signal #9's catalyst keyword fetcher's structural separation from Form-4 XML pipeline. |

| **Spec — §4.4.9 verbatim (`docs/04-modules/longshort/design-source/CROSSWIND_SPEC.md:546-567` — grep-verified)** | <pre>For each catalyst event E for name N in trailing 5 trading days:<br>  catalyst_weight(E) = 3.0 (Tier 1) \| 1.5 (Tier 2) \| 0.5 (Tier 3)<br>  age_weight(E) = exp(-age_in_hours / catalyst_specific_half_life)<br>raw_signal_N = sum(catalyst_weight(E) × age_weight(E))</pre> **Tier 1 (3.0):** Earnings; M&A; FDA approval/rejection/advisory; regulatory action; major guidance update; CEO/CFO change. **Tier 2 (1.5):** Major analyst rating/coverage; material partnership/contract; buyback/dividend change; significant insider transaction; stock split/special dividend. **Tier 3 (0.5):** Minor analyst rating change; conference; non-material product launch; investor day. **Catalyst-specific half-lives (spec-stated):** Earnings 48h; M&A 96h; FDA 72h; Regulatory 96h; Guidance 48h; Executive change 72h; Analyst change 24h; Other Tier 2/3 24–48h. **Active window:** 5 trading days lookback. **Sign:** Unsigned (positive only — direction captured by other signals). **Data source line (verbatim):** "Polygon News + Polygon Reference + SEC EDGAR primary; Benzinga direct, Tradier corporate actions backup." **Missing-data (§4.3.5 non-critical):** `None` when no catalyst events in window → `(value=Decimal('-999'), is_present=0)` to combiner. **Normalization:** within-sector GICS z-score, clipped at ±3. **Cadence (spec):** 5 min during market hours; 15 min after-hours. |

The exact `signal_id` for downstream code is `active_catalyst_flag`. |
| **Architecture branch (NOT YET SELECTED — DEC-057 binding)** | Single-invocation orchestrator for the catalyst-detection layer is plausible: per-call families are all small (calendars ~hundreds to low-thousands of rows trailing-5-days; news single-page ≤1000 items × ~2 pages for a 5-day window per §4.4.8 measured shape) and the per-name z-score normalization is panel-shaped. The branch decision is deferred to Phase 1 because the event-type → vendor binding (B1 table) determines whether the discovery layer is **N calendar-pulls (~6 endpoints, all paged trailing-5-days)** vs **per-symbol fan-out (839 × M endpoint calls)**. The current evidence strongly favors **Branch B+H ("calendars-plus-headline-keywords")**: 6 global calendar pulls (earnings, M&A, dividends, splits, IPO, FDA advisory) ≈ 6 calls × ~200–500ms = 1–3s wall; Polygon news with `insights[]` + `keywords[]` for keyword-derived tiers (CEO change, guidance, regulatory action, partnership) ≈ 2 pages × 6.3s wall ≈ 13s; FMP grades-latest-news feed (reusable from Signal #1) for ratings ≈ 4 calls × 100ms; total ≈ 20s under 150s budget. **Both bounds** (latency + rate) live in the Phase 1 build per FP-047 / FP-048 discipline. |

- `/press-releases?symbol=AAPL&from=&to=`: HTTP **403** — gated.

**Tradier (NOT PROBED this Phase 0)** — the spec data-source line names "Tradier corporate actions backup"; given the strong Polygon + FMP coverage of splits/dividends already evidenced above, Tradier is left as the explicit backup-tier with a DEC-057 §(i) binding to ratify (use only when primary returns `unavailable`).

**Coverage sample (5 names, trailing 5d earnings-occurred + trailing 5w dividends + trailing 5d news with keywords):**
- AAPL: 0 earnings-occurred (next 2026-07-29 forward); 1 ex-dividend 2026-05-11; 50+ news items 7d (insights+keywords populated 100 %).
- MSFT: 0 earnings-occurred (next 2026-07-29 forward); 0 dividends 5w; news coverage ample (FP-048 measured 92 items 7d).
- JPM: 0 earnings-occurred (next 2026-07-14 forward); news coverage moderate.
- JNJ: 0 earnings-occurred (next 2026-07-15 forward).
- KO: 0 earnings-occurred (next 2026-07-28 forward).
→ **None of the 5 sampled names had a trailing-5-day earnings event in this off-cycle slice** (typical for the week before earnings season). The §4.4.9 catalyst-density expectation is correctly low in off-cycle windows; the spec's `None`-when-empty semantic (§4.3.5 non-critical) handles this without fabricated zeros. **Reconciliation with FP-048's 11.4 % news coverage:** that figure was for `insights[]`-derived sentiment scoring; for §4.4.9 catalyst-keyword inference the relevant question is "does at least one news item with a catalyst-keyword appear in trailing 5d per name" — Phase-1 will measure this against the keyword taxonomy ratified in DEC-057 §(b)+§(j).

#### FP-049 §B3 — Gap table + DEC-057 binding skeleton (every binding enumerated for operator ratification)

| Binding | Spec text / question | Proposed v1 binding (NOT YET RATIFIED) | Rationale / honest tradeoff |
|---|---|---|---|
| **§(a) Half-life operational table** | Spec gives "Other Tier 2/3 24–48h" — a range, NOT a value | **Earnings 48h · M&A 96h · FDA 72h · Regulatory 96h · Guidance 48h · Executive change 72h · Analyst change 24h · Partnership 36h · Buyback/Dividend change 36h · Insider transaction 24h · Split/special-dividend 24h · Conference 24h · Product launch 36h · Investor day 36h** | Spec-stated values used verbatim where given (8 of 13). For "Other Tier 2/3" the proposed values pick the midpoint (36h) for genuinely multi-day relevance (partnership/buyback-announcement/launch/investor-day) and the low-end (24h) for single-event point-in-time signals (insider tx / split / conference). Operator may adjust each independently; recorded values become the `CATALYST_HALF_LIFE_HOURS` constant table referenced by the Phase-2 compute function. |
| **§(b) Tier-assignment authority + keyword-inference taxonomy** | Spec lists event types per tier but does NOT say which vendor is authoritative per type, nor a keyword taxonomy for inferred types | **Authority-per-type:** (1) Structured-endpoint = authoritative when available (earnings: FMP; M&A: FMP; ratings: FMP grades; splits/dividends: Polygon; FDA-advisory: Finnhub; corporate-actions backup: Tradier). (2) Keyword-derived secondary when structured is absent (CEO/CFO change, regulatory action, guidance, partnership, buyback announcement, FDA approval/rejection outcome). (3) **Minimum-keyword set per event family with action-verb gate** — published as the `CATALYST_KEYWORDS` lookup in Phase-1 fetcher; values land in DEC-057 §(b) appendix. **Named misclassification risk:** keyword-derived events have higher false-positive rate; the Tier-1/2/3 weighting partially absorbs this (false-positive on a Tier-3 keyword is 0.5 × decay = small contribution); Phase-7 IC ablation will measure whether keyword-derived events should be reweighted. | The action-verb gate is the single highest-impact false-positive control; e.g., "guidance" alone is not an event, but "raises guidance" / "lowers guidance" / "withdraws guidance" + a numeric description-field match is. |
| **§(c) Cross-signal independence flags** | Two §4.4.9 event types overlap with other §4.4.x signals: **Tier-2 "Major analyst rating"** overlaps Signal #1 (analyst_revision_drift, §4.4.5); **Tier-2 "Significant insider transaction"** overlaps Signal #4 (insider transactions, §4.4.4). Same per-event row would be consumed by two signals. | **Question for operator:** do §4.4.5 and §4.4.9 score the SAME analyst-rating row independently (additive double-count is real but acceptable because they measure different things — drift vs flag-presence), OR does #9's "Major analyst rating" event count be GATED off whatever rows #1 has already scored (anti-double-count)? Same question for #4 (insider sized-signal) vs #9 (insider event-occurred flag). **Proposed v1 binding:** INDEPENDENT scoring (additive). Rationale: §4.4.9 measures catalyst-presence (decayed event count, not magnitude); §4.4.5/§4.4.4 measure magnitude (per-event signed). They are orthogonal predictors and additive double-count is the correct combiner-input shape. Operator may override to gated/anti-double-count. | Recording the question is the deliverable; the chosen binding lands in DEC-057 §(c). |
| **§(d) Upcoming-vs-occurred earnings (the discovery axis)** | Spec says "For each catalyst event E for name N in trailing 5 trading days" → ACTIVE catalyst = event that OCCURRED in window. But FMP/Finnhub earnings calendars return BOTH past and future dates (probe evidence above). | **Proposed v1 binding:** OCCURRED-ONLY (forward earnings are NOT a §4.4.9 catalyst; PEAD's #2 covers post-event drift). The operational filter: `event_date ≤ as_of AND event_date ≥ as_of − 5_trading_days`. **However:** the spec is ambiguous on "upcoming-earnings-in-window" (the 1–2 day lead-in is a known pre-earnings drift driver). **Alternative for operator consideration:** include upcoming-within-2-trading-days as Tier-2 (half the weight; "anticipation catalyst"). Lands in DEC-057 §(d). | The Finnhub `hour` enrichment (`bmo`/`amc`) determines decay-origin precision for occurred earnings — set `event_at = date + 09:30 ET (bmo) / 16:00 ET (amc) / 12:00 ET (blank)`. |
| **§(e) Announcement-date vs ex-date (the decay-origin axis)** | For splits/dividend changes: the EVENT for §4.4.9 decay purposes is the **announcement** (`declarationDate` / `declaration_date`) — that's when news hits and price reacts. The ex-date is a separate technical event (price gap, not new info). Probe: FMP `declarationDate` is empty on many calendar rows; Polygon `declaration_date` is more consistently populated. | **Proposed v1 binding:** decay-origin = `declaration_date` (Polygon) for dividends/specials/buyback announcements; fall back to FMP `declarationDate` only when Polygon row absent for the ticker; emit typed skip `declaration_date_unavailable` (counted, not silently dropped) when both are empty rather than substituting ex-date (which would emit a phantom catalyst at the gap). Lands in DEC-057 §(e). | Buyback "announcements" lack a structured source entirely → keyword-derived from press-releases (anchor `published_utc`). |
| **§(f) 5-trading-day → calendar-day window arithmetic** | Spec says "trailing 5 trading days" — needs a calendar conversion rule + holiday handling. | **Proposed v1 binding:** trading-day calendar = NYSE business-day calendar (per `pandas_market_calendars` shape, but implemented as a calendar-aware date stepper over `signal_compute_log.as_of_date`); 5 trading days back ≈ 7 calendar days mid-week, 8–9 days across a weekend or holiday week. The orchestrator computes `window_start_at = nthPrecedingTradingDay(as_of, 5)` and emits both bounds in `signal_compute_log` metadata for replay determinism. Lands in DEC-057 §(f). | Aligns with §4.4.5 / §4.4.8's calendar-day windows; the 5-trading-day vs 7-calendar-day difference is small but matters for events landing on weekends (e.g., Friday-evening guidance announcement scored on Monday). |
| **§(g) v1 event-type subset under current entitlement (the §(k)-rider deviation)** | Some event types have no structured source at our four-vendor entitlement and would be entirely keyword-derived. | **Proposed v1 binding — IN at v1:** Earnings (FMP), M&A (FMP), Splits (Polygon), Dividends/declarations (Polygon), Ratings (FMP grades), FDA-advisory (Finnhub), Executive-change (FMP key-executives diff + news keyword), Guidance (news keyword + action-verb gate), Regulatory action (news keyword + entity gate), Partnership (news keyword). **OUT at v1 (DEC-057 §(g) named deviation):** FDA approval/rejection outcomes (no structured source; keyword-only too noisy without an entity-gate to drug names); Buyback announcements (no structured source; defer to v2 when keyword-derivation taxonomy proves stable); Conferences (Tier 3; high false-positive rate; defer); Non-material product launches (Tier 3; high false-positive rate; defer); Investor day (Tier 3; defer with conferences). **Spec compliance:** §(k)-rider names the OUT-set explicitly; the v1 signal computes against the IN-set; the OUT-set is a deferred-work entry, NOT a silent drop. | Operator may flip any OUT to IN with a keyword taxonomy update — the architecture supports incremental addition. |
| **§(h) Source-of-truth precedence + cross-vendor dedup** | The same event may appear on two vendors (e.g., Polygon news + FMP press-release for the same partnership). Need a dedup key. | **Proposed v1 binding:** dedup key = `(ticker, event_type, event_at[rounded to 1-hour bucket])`. First-occurrence-wins (the INC-74 dedupe discipline). Vendor precedence per event-type table above (structured > keyword-derived). Lands in DEC-057 §(h). | Cross-vendor news dedup beyond the §4.4.8 publisher set is a known hazard; the 1-hour bucket is conservative (an event hitting two wires within an hour collapses to one catalyst — correct semantics). |
| **§(i) Tradier corporate-actions backup binding** | Spec names Tradier as backup; not probed this Phase 0. | **Proposed v1 binding:** Tradier consulted ONLY when Polygon `/v3/reference/splits` OR `/v3/reference/dividends` returns `unavailable` for the as_of window (typed-error path). No primary Tradier consumption at v1. Phase-1 builds the Tradier fetcher behind the same shared-fetcher shape (unavailable path returns typed enum, never silent empty). Lands in DEC-057 §(i). | Keeps Tradier in the surface area as the spec requires without paying its latency cost on every fire. |
| **§(j) Minimum-keyword taxonomy per event family** | Spec mentions keyword inference only by implication; the taxonomy is operator-ratifiable. | **Proposed v1 binding (the IN-set from §(g) only):** see B1 table per-row "keywords" columns. Codified as `CATALYST_KEYWORDS: Record<EventType, ReadonlyArray<string>>` in Phase-1 fetcher; the action-verb subset (§(b)) is a SEPARATE map `CATALYST_VERB_GATE: Record<EventType, ReadonlyArray<string>>`. Both lookups land verbatim in `docs/04-modules/longshort/signals/active-catalyst-flag.md` for operator audit at Phase-1 commit. | The keyword list is the single highest-volatility binding (operator may refine after Phase-1 false-positive measurement); v1 starts conservative + narrow. |

#### FP-049 — Forbidden (this PR, re-stated for the record)

No Signal-#4 / EDGAR work this PR. No compute / fetcher / orchestrator builds for #9. | **Phase-0b addendum — Finnhub news cross-probe (live, 2026-06-11, 8 of 15 calls; $0 — existing Estimate-1 entitlement)** | **Motivation:** Phase-0 found Polygon's pool = 4 publishers, zero tier-1/2 — the §4.4.8 tier-weighting reduces to a near-constant under our entitlement. Before ratifying DEC-056 bindings, cross-probe Finnhub (the paid PEAD vendor) to determine whether its `/company-news` surface is materially richer (tier-1/2 presence, coverage density). **Probe scaffolding:** transient `supabase/functions/probe-fp048b-finnhub/index.ts` deployed for live access (Supabase secret `FINNHUB_API_KEY` reachable only from deployed runtime); deployed → 8 probe calls executed → **DELETED in this same PR** per investigation-only scaffolding discipline (FP-047 / FP-048 Phase-0 precedent). **Calls (8 of 15 budget):** 1 entitlement+shape (AAPL trailing 7d) + 6 publisher pool (AAPL, NVDA, JPM, CAT, NKE, MA trailing 7d) + 1 `/news-sentiment?symbol=AAPL`. **Raw evidence (verbatim from probe response):**<br>**(1) Entitlement + shape.** `GET /company-news?symbol=AAPL&from=2026-06-04&to=2026-06-11` → HTTP 200, 150068 bytes, **244 articles in 7d** (≈4× the Polygon `ticker=AAPL` count of 63). Per-article keys: `category, datetime, headline, id, image, related, source, summary, url`. **No per-article sentiment field** (`sentiment`, `sentiment_score`, `score` all absent — confirmed honestly). Sample item (1 of 3): `{category:"company", datetime:1781191380, headline:"Why Intel Stock Was Just Upgraded to Buy from Sell at BofA", id:140607045, related:"AAPL", source:"Yahoo", summary:"Intel stock is double-upgraded to Buy from Underperform at BofA Securities…", url:"https://finnhub.io/api/news?id=786e510f…"}`.<br>**(2) Publisher pool (6 symbols, 873 total articles trailing 7d):** `Yahoo` 677 (77.5 %), `Benzinga` 73 (8.4 %), `CNBC` 46 (5.3 %), `SeekingAlpha` 43 (4.9 %), `ChartMill` 33 (3.8 %), `Fintel` 1. **CNBC PRESENT — first tier-2 publisher seen at any entitlement we hold.** Tier-1 (Reuters/Bloomberg/WSJ/FT/Dow Jones) and remaining tier-2 (Forbes/Barron's/NYT) ABSENT in this probe slice. Press-release wires (GlobeNewswire / BusinessWire / PR Newswire / Accesswire) **NOT seen** in the 873-item Finnhub slice (vs 37 % of the Polygon feed) — Finnhub appears to pre-filter PR wires upstream.<br>**(3) `/news-sentiment?symbol=AAPL`:** HTTP **403 — `{"error":"You don't have access to this resource."}`** — premium-gated at our Estimate-1 entitlement. The pre-scored aggregate (buzz / bullish-bearish %) is NOT available without an entitlement upgrade. Finding, not failure.<br>**(4) Timestamp + dedup.** `datetime` is unix seconds (all sampled values in [1.78e9, 1.79e9] — verified integer-seconds range), precision sufficient for the §4.4.8 hourly `exp(−age_hours/24)` decay. **39 articles appear under ≥2 symbols** in the 873-item slice (cross-symbol dedup count = 39; e.g. id=140572134 appeared under AAPL+CAT+NKE; id=140582052 under AAPL+CAT+NKE). The `related` field on the per-symbol endpoint **returns ONLY the queried symbol** (single-ticker string, not a multi-ticker list) — so the article's full ticker-set is only recoverable by per-symbol enumeration + de-dup-by-id (NOT a per-(article,ticker) sentiment shape — sentiment-less in the first place). **The per-(article,ticker) sentiment shape that Polygon `insights[]` provides cleanly is absent from Finnhub.**<br>**(5) Press-release presence:** zero PR-wire publishers in the 873-item slice — `source` field would identify them by name (e.g. "GlobeNewswire") if present; Finnhub appears to source from media aggregators (Yahoo) and direct news rooms (CNBC, Benzinga, SeekingAlpha) only. PR exclusion is structurally unnecessary at this entitlement (but the deny-list pattern remains forward-compatible). |

| **Phase-0b recommendation (the deliverable — for operator decision)** | **Recommendation: Polygon — vendor-of-record for Signal #8 v1.** Rationale: the §4.4.8 formula REQUIRES per-(article,ticker) sentiment; Polygon delivers it at 100 % presence; Finnhub does not deliver any per-article sentiment at all. Choosing Finnhub for v1 would force **FinBERT-in-v1** (the spec's named v2 fallback pulled into v1 build cost): adds an LLM inference path to the compute, an additional ~150-200 MB model artifact, GPU/CPU cost per article, and an entirely new gate surface (model-version drift, model-output validation). That is a material build cost the spec only budgeted as a v2 fallback contingent on Polygon insights-coverage failure — and Polygon coverage at v1 entitlement is 100 %. Finnhub's coverage-density advantage (~4× more articles) and CNBC tier-2 presence are real but do NOT compensate for losing the sentiment primitive — within-sector z-score normalizes amplitude, so denser-but-sentiment-less is strictly worse than sparser-but-sentiment-present. **Hybrid (Finnhub pool + FinBERT scoring vs Polygon pre-scored) is technically buildable but rejected for v1** on the same FinBERT-pull-in-cost grounds. Hybrid is the natural v2 path IF Polygon insights coverage degrades or operator wants strength-scoring (the v2 LLM enhancement-arc). **Action for operator:** confirm Polygon-as-vendor (no DEC-056 reshape needed — existing bindings stand) OR override with Finnhub-as-vendor (forces DEC-056 §(a)+(g) reshape to specify FinBERT-in-v1 with model-version pin, model-card, drift-monitoring contract — material additional scope; recommend a separate FP-048b for that path if chosen). |
