### FP-067-ADD-01 — W1 pre-build defect-list correction (Rule 8 additive, ACT-432, 2026-06-30)

**Charter preserved verbatim above.** This ADD appends a correction under the pointer; it does NOT edit the charter body.

**What the charter got wrong.** The ACT-431 charter listed 3 defects on `combiner_shap_attribution`. The supervisor↔Lovable W1 pre-build reconciliation (grep + LIVE constraint verification via `information_schema` / `pg_constraint` at HEAD `6611efe9`) **REFUTES defect (1)** ("not slot-rotated → stale FK to slot-rotated `combiner_rankings`"). Migration `20260626190957` ("FP-057 Sub-step 1 (1/5) — combiner_rankings: additive intraday_slot superset PK rotation") fully rotated `combiner_shap_attribution` in the same transaction as `combiner_rankings`: added `intraday_slot`, dropped the old FK, rotated the PK to `(operator_id, as_of_date, ticker, intraday_slot)`, and re-created the FK to `combiner_rankings(operator_id, as_of_date, ticker, intraday_slot)` — the slot-rotated key. `src/integrations/supabase/types.ts` + live `pg_constraint` confirm: PK is the 4-tuple, FK is on the rotated quadruple with `ON DELETE CASCADE`. The table IS slot-rotated; the FK is NOT stale. The charter was written against a pre-rotation read of the base migration `20260616103102…:205-217` and missed the 06-26 rotation.

**Corrected (true) defect list — TWO, not three:**
- (1) **REFUTED** — slot-rotated + FK correct since 2026-06-26 (MIG at `supabase/migrations/20260626190957…`). No W1 work here.
- (2) **NO WRITER — STANDS.** Zero producers across `supabase/functions/**`; the fallback path never writes `attributions`.
- (3) **NO `side` COLUMN — STANDS.** Live columns confirmed: `operator_id, as_of_date, ticker, attributions, model_id, computed_at, intraday_slot` — no `side`. Register item 2.1 needs per-side decomposition.

**Reconciled W1 scope (shrinks from 3-defect-fix to 2):** (a) add a `side` dimension, (b) fallback per-signal decomposition writer. The slot-rotation work the charter implied is **ALREADY DONE** — W1 does NOT touch `intraday_slot` or the `combiner_rankings` FK's slot component. Charter §Acceptance Criteria row item (1) "W1 migration re-rotates … PK, re-scopes FK" reads correctly IF interpreted as the `side`-driven rotation below; it does NOT re-do slot rotation.

**Side-column design fork (the one real W1 design decision, operator-notable):**
- **Option A — FIRST-CLASS `side` (recommended).** Rotate PK to the 5-tuple `(operator_id, as_of_date, ticker, intraday_slot, side)` and re-scope the self-FK; per-side means up to 2 rows per quadruple. Costs a PK/FK migration on a money-path-adjacent table (additive superset rotation, mirrors the 06-26 slot rotation's shape). Gives the register-2.2 per-side-IC probe a clean join with no jsonb-extract.
- **Option B — jsonb-nest.** Keep PK at 4-tuple; nest `{long:{...}, short:{...}}` inside `attributions`. NO migration, but every FP-058 per-side read-view must jsonb-extract, partly defeating the clean-substrate purpose of item 2.1.
- **Supervisor + charter recommendation: Option A (first-class).** Item 2.1's whole purpose is per-side decomposition; jsonb-nesting pushes extraction gymnastics into every downstream read.
- **Resolution: operator-gated at W1 authorization.** Default is Option A per the recommendation unless operator prefers the no-migration jsonb-nest.

**W1 readiness.** Unblocked. True scope is (a) `side` dimension (first-class PK rotation, pending operator nod on the fork) + (b) writer. Nothing else on the table blocks W1: RLS is service-role-only-write (already correct for a sidecar writer keyed by service-role); the `attributions jsonb` shape has only the object-typeof CHECK (compatible with either fork option); `model_id` FK to `combiner_model_registry` intact.

**Cross-references (ADD-01):** ACT-432 (this correction); FP-067 charter (preserved above); migration `20260626190957` (the load-bearing rotation the charter missed); live `pg_constraint` read at HEAD `6611efe9` (verdict grounding); ROI-attribution register item 2.1 (unchanged — capture shape now derives from the true 2-defect list); Constitution Rule 8 (charter preserved, correction additive).

### FP-067-ADD-02 — Option A ratified; W1 built and closed (Rule 8 additive, ACT-433, 2026-07-01)

**Charter + ADD-01 preserved verbatim above.** This ADD records the operator-ratified resolution of the ADD-01 side-column design fork and the W1 build outcome. Charter/ADD-01 body NOT edited — recorded under-pointer per Rule 8.

**Fork resolution — Option A (first-class `side`) RATIFIED.** Operator green-light at the W1 authorization gate. Rationale: (i) `side` is the primary read-partition of every FP-058 attribution query — jsonb-nesting inverts substrate/payload and forces `->>` extraction on every downstream read; (ii) the 4→5-tuple additive superset PK rotation is the same shape as the 06-26 4-tuple rotation on this exact table (which landed cleanly per ADD-01), so the migration risk is fully characterized; (iii) the pre-writer window is the cheapest possible time to rotate — zero live producers to coordinate with, zero readers to migrate.

**Backfill source correction (grounding).** The charter's implied backfill source `combiner_rankings.side` **does not exist** (`combiner_rankings` PK is `(op, as_of, ticker, intraday_slot)` with `long_rank`/`short_rank` columns — no `side` column). Live `pg_constraint` read confirms `combiner_book` carries the side attribution: UNIQUE `(operator_id, as_of_date, ticker, intraday_slot)` with a `side text CHECK IN ('long','short')` column. **MIG-150 backfills from `combiner_book` on the (op, as_of, ticker, slot) join key.** Unattributable orphans (shap rows with no matching book row) are **DELETED** (typed handling; no fabricated `side`) — expected to affect zero rows because no writer had ever populated `combiner_shap_attribution` before W1.

**MIG-150 shape (applied 2026-07-01, ledger entry same PR):** (i) `ADD COLUMN side text` NULLABLE (backfill window); (ii) `UPDATE ... FROM combiner_book` on the 4-tuple; (iii) `DELETE ... WHERE side IS NULL` (typed handling of orphans); (iv) `ALTER COLUMN side SET NOT NULL` + `CHECK (side IN ('long','short'))`; (v) `DROP CONSTRAINT ..._pkey; ADD PRIMARY KEY (op, as_of, ticker, slot, side)`. **Self-FK to `combiner_rankings` UNCHANGED** (rankings has no `side` — the FK stays on the rotated 4-tuple; `side` is NOT added to the FK). Fully additive (D2), guarded/idempotent (D3), scoped to combiner (D4), ledgered (D5). Post-apply live verification: PK = `(operator_id, as_of_date, ticker, intraday_slot, side)`; FK to `combiner_rankings(operator_id, as_of_date, ticker, intraday_slot) ON DELETE CASCADE` intact; CHECK `side = ANY (ARRAY['long','short'])` present.

**W1 writer built.** `supabase/functions/_shared/longshort-combiner/shap-attribution-writer.ts`. Pure `decomposeFallbackComposite(row)` returns `{composite, present_count, contributions}` where `contribution_i = raw_i / max(1, presentCount)` — byte-identical arithmetic to `ranker.ts` `computeComposite`. R2 (typed-nothing, not fabricated 0): gated-null critical signals (DEC-071 3c) and absent-non-critical signals (`is_present=0/null`) are OMITTED from the contributions map — never emitted as `0`. R4 (Σ == composite): the linear reconstruction is EXACT by construction (`Σ raw_i / denom = numerator / denom = composite`); unit test `shap-attribution-writer_test.ts` asserts `assertAlmostEquals(Σ contributions, composite, 1e-12)` on fully-present, absent-non-critical, and gated-null-critical fixtures. R3 (injected clock): `computed_at` derived via `productionClock.getWallClockTs()` (top-of-call-chain sanctioned exception per `longshort-clock.ts` DEC-034 (4)); test path can inject `createFixedClock`. Bug-null critical (not in `gated_signals`) throws `ShapDecompositionInvariantError` — invariant preserved, mirrors `IncludedRowInvariantError`.

**W1 sidecar wire (fire-and-forget, ACT-419 precedent).** Wired in `ranker-orchestrator.ts` AFTER the `combiner_book` UPSERT completes, BEFORE the transition-audit step, gated on `ranker_source_literal === RANKER_SOURCE_FALLBACK` (LGBM branch is W2, not W1). Wrapped in `try { await writeFallbackShapAttributions(...) } catch (shapErr) { console.error(...) }` — **R1 held: the writer CANNOT throw into the orchestrator's return payload; failure is caught, logged, swallowed.** One row per booked name per (op, as_of, slot, side) — combiner_book UNIQUE guarantees one row per name, matching the charter constraint.

**R-invariants verification (post-build).** R1: sidecar wrapped in try/catch at the orchestrator wire; grep-confirmed no code path where writer failure propagates into the orchestrator result. R2: contributions map OMITS gated-null and absent-non-critical (unit tests lock this — no `hasOwnProperty` for absent keys). R3: `productionClock.getWallClockTs()` used; no raw `Date.now()` or bare `new Date()` in the writer module. R4: Σ contributions == composite by algebraic construction + `assertAlmostEquals(..., 1e-12)` unit test.

**STOP-conditions honored:** (a) backfill sources `combiner_book.side` (NOT `combiner_rankings.side` which doesn't exist); (b) MIG-150 additive-only (no column DROP, no data loss beyond the DELETE-of-typed-orphans which is documented and honest); (c) MIG-150 idempotent (guarded ADD/CHECK/PK); (d) self-FK to `combiner_rankings` UNCHANGED (no `side` on the FK); (e) writer fire-and-forget with try/catch at the wire (R1); (f) no fabricated zeros for gated/absent signals (R2); (g) no raw `Date.now()` (R3); (h) decomposition sums exactly to composite (R4); (i) both `deno.lock` files remain at v5; (j) LGBM branch UNTOUCHED — W2 scope preserved.

**Cross-references (ADD-02):** ACT-433 (this build); FP-067-ADD-01 (defect-list correction this ADD-02 executes against); MIG-150 (`docs/07-reference/database-migration-ledger.md`); `supabase/functions/_shared/longshort-combiner/shap-attribution-writer.ts` (pure decomposer + sidecar); `supabase/functions/_shared/longshort-combiner/shap-attribution-writer_test.ts` (R2/R4 unit tests); `supabase/functions/_shared/longshort-combiner/ranker-orchestrator.ts` (fire-and-forget wire, fallback-branch-only gate); `supabase/functions/_shared/longshort-combiner/ranker.ts` `computeComposite` (the linear formula the decomposition inverts); DEC-071 sub-step 3c (gated-null skip mirrored); DEC-074 (`SIGNAL_IDS_FALLBACK_SUM` order); DEC-070 clause (h).2 (additive-substrate pattern); ACT-419 (fire-and-forget sidecar precedent); Constitution Rule 8 (charter + ADD-01 preserved, ADD-02 additive).

### FP-069 — OVERSHOOT: acute-dislocation mean-reversion event strategy (separate account, broker-first)

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| **Status** | execution-in-progress. **W0** chartered at ACT-454. **W1a** landed at ACT-455 (2026-07-03): 4 `overshoot_*` tables live with RLS (RESTRICTIVE deny-writes + `overshoot.view`-gated SELECT); `overshoot.view` + `overshoot.manage` permissions seeded and granted to Administrator; `overshoot_universe` seeded to 839 rows from `universe_membership`; `PolygonDailyOhlcvFetcher` + `FinnhubEarningsFetcher` + `FmpEarningsCalendarFetcher` shipped in `_shared/overshoot/`; `overshoot-backfill-bars-manual` + `overshoot-backfill-earnings-manual` edge functions deployed; separation-contract CI guard active in `.github/workflows/overshoot-guards.yml` (5/5 guard tests green, 10/10 fetcher tests green, guard scan clean on real tree). **W1b** pending: full 839-ticker bars backfill + 5y Finnhub earnings backfill + full-universe Finnhub↔FMP cross-audit — executor-driven under a separate continuation prompt; W1 does NOT close at ACT-455. **W2-W5** unchanged. |
| **Tier** | A — future money-adjacent system. This turn is documentation-only. |
| **Mode** | DOCUMENTATION-ONLY at charter. |
| **Origin** | Operator-authorized 2026-07-02 post-DW-212 short-book forensic. Overshoot is the natural counterpart to the reversal-effect the current long-short book was shorting into (i.e., already-crashed names bouncing against a flat SPY). |
| **Thesis** | Capture short-horizon mean reversion after acute idiosyncratic dislocations. **LONG tail:** 12-1-strong names that drop acutely (band parameter-selected, prior ~6-12% over 1-5 trading days), NO earnings/catalyst in window, market/sector flat, stabilization-trigger entry, scale-out or time-stop exit. **SHORT tail (mirror):** 12-1-weak names that pop acutely, same exclusions PLUS a hard high-short-interest squeeze filter, fade on exhaustion, cover on retrace/time-stop. Capacity up to 20 per side, filled as events qualify (variable inventory; occupancy is a **measured output**, never a target). Each tail earns its slots on evidence independently — a long-tilted outcome is acceptable and expected under regimes where short-side qualification is sparse. |
| **Motivating evidence (DW-212)** | 2026-07-02 short-book forensic: 74% of the live short leg's ~$1,022 / 44h loss came from 6 names > 40% below 52-week highs bouncing against a flat SPY (−0.135%); ~100% idiosyncratic (residual after SPY beta); reversal-1w sanctioned-null for all 16 shorts made the entry a one-signal momentum book that structurally shorted already-crashed names. Overshoot harvests the OTHER side of that effect. The >40%-drawdown bucket is a **tested dimension**, not an assumption; the naive "long everything 40% off highs" version has negative expected edge (the band must be parameter-selected against event-arrival-rate + slippage-haircut). |
| **SEPARATION CONTRACT (binding, preserve verbatim)** | (1) **BROKER** — a SECOND, dedicated Alpaca paper account; new secrets (`ALPACA_PAPER_KEY_OVERSHOOT` / `ALPACA_PAPER_SECRET_OVERSHOOT`, operator-provisioned via Dashboard at Wave 3 per §22.5.3); new client instance; the INC-77 paper-only-guard PATTERN reused, existing client code never modified. Rationale: the longshort rebalance is broker-sourced and closes any account position outside its target book — a shared account means systematic liquidation of overshoot positions. (2) **DATABASE** — reads shared strategy-agnostic market facts (`signal_observations`, the Wave-1 commons); writes ONLY `overshoot_`-prefixed tables; never writes any `longshort_` / `combiner_` row. **PERFORMANCE-LEDGER PRINCIPLE (operator-ratified):** the ONLY performance numbers overshoot ever reports derive from broker (paper) fills in `overshoot_fills` / `overshoot_positions`; any study/parameter artifact lives in `overshoot_study_`-prefixed tables, labeled NON-PERFORMANCE, structurally never merged into any performance surface. (3) **CODE** — parallel tree (`supabase/functions/_shared/overshoot/` + `overshoot-*` edge fns); may import leaf utilities (clock, Polygon fetcher, z-score helpers); MUST NOT import anything under `longshort-execution/`; no existing file gains an import from the overshoot tree; CI-grep-enforced from Wave 1. (4) **CRON** — `overshoot.*` jobs in free slots, disarmed at creation; the live strategy's jobids (51/76/78/87/88/89/90/91/95/97/100/106/109/110/114) untouched. (5) **DOCS** — spec lives in `docs/04-modules/overshoot/`; `CROSSWIND_SPEC.md` and all current-strategy sections: zero edits; project FP/DW/ACT ledgers are shared bookkeeping only. |
| **BORN-WITH-THE-LESSONS (binding on all overshoot build waves)** | Paper-only URL guard on the overshoot client (INC-77 pattern); injected clock, zero raw wall-clock (DEC-034 discipline); typed absence, no sentinel numerics; audit events carry `outcome_class` from v1 (`refused_*` / `submitted` / `no_op` — the DW-208 lesson, built-in not retrofitted); event-idempotency keys on the detector (one dislocation → one event); disarm-fire-enable convention on every overshoot cron; overshoot-own `verify_position`-class reconciliation against its own account from the first trading wave; producer/consumer cadence coherence verified to fixed-point at design time (the DW-208 / DW-210 / DW-211 lesson). |
| **Wave ladder** | **W0** — charter (this turn). **W1** — data commons: overshoot-owned MIGs for a daily-bars table + ~5y Polygon backfill (~2,500 tickers, adjusted; `PolygonPriceHistoryFetcher` exists as an importable leaf utility) + a historical earnings-calendar table + backfill (THE load-bearing exclusion filter — the strategy cannot separate drift from reversion without it; source chosen by dual-investigation at W1) + the CI import-guard enforcing the separation contract. **W2 [DECISION-GATED at W1 close, operator call]** — historical parameter-selection study (grid: drop/pop % × window × momentum-quintile × 52w-drawdown-bucket × earnings-exclusion; conservative slippage haircuts baked in; outputs = parameter choices + event-arrival rates ONLY, `overshoot_study_` tables, non-performance by construction). Operator may KEEP (front-loads parameter learning) or DROP (literature priors, tuned on paper evidence). **W3** — broker-first execution build: live event detector over the W1 commons + entry/exit engine + `overshoot_` ledger fed by REAL paper fills on account #2 + own reconciliation + outcome-classified audit from v1 + operator pre-condition (account #2 creation + two secrets). **W4** — operator console UI (own tree, read-only, injected clock): the event-detector monitor (dislocations detected, per-filter pass/fail with reasons, entries vs skips — the operator's window into whether the strategy sees the market correctly) + positions/closed-today/equity on account #2 (FP-068 lesson-set, patterns reused not imported). **W5** — measurement + scale decision on real paper numbers only. |
| **Broker-first rationale (operator-ratified)** | Simulated fills are derivative numbers with no external prime (supervisor-instructions §2 axiom 2), a phantom-number class, and a sim-to-real migration liability. Broker-first (from the strategy's FIRST forward trading day) yields one fill path + one broker-truth ledger from day one. Consequence: no offline paper-sim harness is chartered; the W2 study, if kept, emits parameter choices only, not fills. |
| **Constraints (STOP-conditions)** | Any shared write-surface with longshort (writing to `longshort_` / `combiner_` from overshoot code) → STOP. Any import from `longshort-execution/` into `supabase/functions/_shared/overshoot/` → STOP (CI-guarded from W1). Any overshoot cron created ARMED → STOP. Any performance number reported from an `overshoot_study_` table → STOP (constitutional per performance-ledger principle). Any silent numeric sentinel in an overshoot money path → STOP (born-with-lessons). Any raw wall-clock in an overshoot kernel → STOP. Any use of a shared Alpaca client instance for overshoot → STOP. |
